# Library map

Generated by `tools/progress/gen.py` from `tools/progress/data/` (refresh with `tools/progress/sync.py`). Upstream: TomBox `7e8064f`; emulator source paths link to upstream. Titles analysed: Fortnite (console OS 10.0.19041.3948), XDefiant (console OS 10.0.22000.4429).

## Statuses

| Status | Meaning |
|---|---|
| implemented | the emulator provides its own implementation |
| forwarded | an emulator DLL exports it and forwards to a PC system component (D3D12, Media Foundation, DirectStorage) |
| hooked | resolved from a PC system DLL; the emulator patches the entry point at run time (network policy) |
| pc | a PC System32 DLL of the same name exports the same name; resolving is not the same as behaving like the console |
| title | provided by a DLL that ships with the title |
| stub | the emulator exports it but returns a fixed result (E_NOTIMPL / S_OK / inert object) |
| unverified | not observed loading, or an ordinal export whose console meaning was not checked |
| missing | no emulator export and no PC export of that name |

Shader compile: all 139 shaders of the lobby frame compile with DXC (TomBox `docs/DEVELOPMENT_LOG.md:894`); 25/25 fixtures compile (`:672`). The full-library compile sweep (`:706`) has no published final count, so no compile badge is shown.

Direct3D 12.x: 160 / 128 are the table sizes the wrappers allocate (`d3d12_xs/src/device_wrap.rs`, `cmdlist_wrap.rs`), not documented interface sizes. Device: done = forwarded PC slots 0-43 plus intercepted Xbox slots 44, 48, 55, 60-62, 73, 78-81; partial = logging S_OK stubs 45-90. CommandList: done = 0-26, 32-52 (PC methods shifted by 4), 59, 67, 68, 83; 27-31 are Xbox-inserted stubs and 53-127 log-and-ignore probes, counted as not done.

## Fortnite imports (2.0.1461.7919)

Every static import of the EXE and its statically loaded DLLs resolved at load: the process booted and presented frames in the 2026-11-21 runs. Delay imports resolve only when first called.

XDefiant import totals (11 binaries): implemented 8, forwarded 1, hooked 25, pc 1216, title 342, stub 4, missing 16.

## Xbox Series system libraries (EmbeddedXvd, union of all analysed titles)

Every export of every DLL in each title's EmbeddedXvd `Windows\System32` and `System32\forwarders` (union, so the newest console OS defines the surface), plus the GDK wrappers the emulator redirects inside the Fortnite EXE (statically linked, not exports; build-specific). `pc` means a PC DLL of the same name exports the same name. The emulator's `mfplat.dll` proxy shadows the PC one for the title, so console mfplat exports it does not forward are counted missing. xgameruntime.dll exports only its dispatcher entry points; the GDK services behind it (XGameSave, XGameUi, XNetworking, ...) are reached via `QueryApiImpl` and are not enumerated here. Import attribution for API-set imports picks one host DLL (KernelBase, ucrtbase, ntdll, combase, ... first).

## Unverified

- Fortnite: GameChat2.dll (14 delay imports) and everything it imports: never seen loading. CONCRT140.dll (MSVCP140 delay import): not observed.
- XAudio2_9 / GameInput ordinal imports: the PC ordinal names are listed; that the console DLL uses the same ordinals was not checked. XAudio2 starts streaming in the Fortnite log, but there is no audio.
- `pc` status is an export-name match only; behaviour differences between console and PC system DLLs are not measured.
- Console exports that exist only by ordinal on the PC DLL are `unverified`.
- Titles without run evidence are classified statically only.
- Milestones 14/19 and the RDNA2 instruction table are upstream's numbers (`progress.json`, `data/mnemonics.txt`); not re-measured here.
